- ZiDAWGGetGraphInfo: all three initial distinct attempts logged, plus followups where indicated. Initial objdiff 81.83582%, instruction-exact count retained.

- zidawg1: extab and extabindex bytes now identical, including all four owned exception records. No metadata renames or extent changes retained.
- zi8pud2: extab and extabindex bytes now identical; explicit 16-bit section-count/offset decoding follows four target clrlwi instructions. No metadata changes.
- zi8uwd: no literal/global data. Residual extab record for Zi8MatchUWDdata saves r19-r31 in target, r21-r31 in source; extabindex length is target 1984 versus source 2096. These are real code-generation differences, not name/extent errors. Other three extab records and their lengths match.
- ziswordw: .sdata2 and extabindex bytes identical; residual extab for Zi8IsWordW saves r26-r31 in target, r27-r31 in source. Zi8ConvertUC2UserKey unwind bytes and instruction-exact function remain unchanged. The residual is code register allocation, not a data initializer or symbol-name mismatch.
- No symbol-size adjustments, forced sections, alignment objects, volatile casts, or assembly added. All retained code is in owned units.
